08  地狱之船 / Waterway to Hell

Waterway to Hell
地狱之船

Aboard the "Alligator"
“短吻鳄”号游轮

The wind blows through Lopera's hair.
风穿过少女的指缝。

The tourists gather at the ship's edge, watching the locals in small boats approaching the cruise ship.
外国游客聚集在船舷边，看向那些划着小艇靠近游轮的当地住民。

Cruise Passenger: A few treats to buy their worship. And they call it "kindness." How undignified, to be reduced to begging for scraps like a dog.
轮船游客：他们从没关心过圣保罗的穷人，像给动物园的猴子投喂香蕉，打发一点“善心”。

The children on the boats wave their hands and shout in an attempt to attract the tourists' attention.
船上小孩挥舞双手，发出喊叫吸引游客注意。

The crew and onboard priest have packed supplies for the tourists to buy and throw to the impoverished children—all part of the tour experience.
船员与随船牧师打包好了可供抛掷的施舍物，兜售给每一位乐意参与这一“游赏项目”的客人。

Lopera: Dignity doesn't count for much in the face of hunger.
洛佩拉：这些“香蕉”养活了不少人，朋友。吃饱饭比什么都要紧，管他是哪来的饭，反正拉出来都一样。

The stranger nods, though he doesn't seem to completely agree.
陌生游客点点头，但他似乎并不完全同意这一点。

Cruise Passenger: Yet no man can live by bread alone.
轮船游客：人不单靠食物活着。

Lopera: And yet none can live without it.
洛佩拉：人不靠食物就没法活着。

Cruise Passenger: You're a clever girl, Miss. But I hear from your accent you are no local. So, what brings you to São Paulo? Business, right? I have some business myself in the favelas.
轮船游客：您也去圣保罗做买卖吗？我在贫民区有些生意。

Lopera: Colombia, sir, and no, only a visit. To the São Paulo Veterans' Residence. It's a safe place. If you find yourself in trouble, you should seek it out.
洛佩拉：买卖？不，我回家，到圣保罗那间荣军院，如果你在城里找不到住处，朋友，上那儿来，我帮你。

Cruise Passenger: Oh, is it now? Thank you. You're very kind to offer.
轮船游客：感谢您的好意。

Lopera idly tosses a small die up in the air and catches it in her hand again.
洛佩拉托着下巴，无聊地抛掷着小骰子，投起又落下，落下再投起……

The stranger's eyes follow the die as it traces its path.
游客的目光随骰子上下晃动。

Cruise Passenger: Care for a game of dice, little lady?
轮船游客：要来玩一把吗，女士？

Lopera: What's the bet?
洛佩拉：赌注是什么？

She glances at the black suitcase by the stranger's feet.
她瞄向游客手边的黑色旅行箱。

Cruise Passenger: Well, let us bet against the goodness in one another.
轮船游客：赌你我的善心吧。

Cruise Passenger: Let us say that the loser will cover the cost of all the food given to all the little vendors today,
轮船游客：输的人为孩子们买下这艘船的施舍。

Cruise Passenger: in the winner's name, of course.
轮船游客：然后，以赢家的名义布施。

A generous gamble for a conscience that knocks on heaven's door.
一场慷慨的赌局，为了叩响天上门扉的良心。

Lopera: That's no small wager, sir. Still, I appreciate a good game.
洛佩拉：你这人还挺有意思。那来吧，飞——

The stranger smiles as he watches Lopera drop the die on the table and shake it in the wooden cup.
陌生的客人微笑着摊开双手，看着骰子从洛佩拉手中掷出，又像雨滴般落回桌面，在木杯里摇晃。

Lopera: High-low then? Who goes first?
洛佩拉：你先还是我先？一二三小，四五六大。

Cruise Passenger: Ladies first.
轮船游客：您请。

Lopera: Low.
洛佩拉：小。

Cruise Passenger: Let's make it more interesting. Six. If it rolls anything else, you win.
轮船游客：六。否则都算您赢。

She shakes the cup again, the die unevenly tumbling around inside.
木杯再一次晃起，一枚“不体面”的骰子旋转着，将胜利带给其主人。

Cruise Passenger: Do you mind?
轮船游客：请。

Cruise Passenger: I lost, just as was meant to be. So then, I am to be the vehicle of grace today.
轮船游客：看来我输了，是神有意让我为他不幸的孩子们做点什么。

Cruise Passenger: Might I have your name, Miss? So that all may know the name, which is to be sung in paradise today.
轮船游客：请告诉我您的名字，以便为孩子们布施时能让天园响唱起您的名字。

Lopera: Carlotta Lopez Rivera. But it is "Lopera" should you ask after me.
洛佩拉：卡洛塔·洛佩兹·里维拉，但如果你要找我，就打听“洛佩拉”。

Lopera. Lopera of São Paulo.
洛佩拉，圣保罗的洛佩拉。

The defeated stranger picks up the die. He notices something in its design and cracks a smile.
落败的游客拿起骰子，笑着察觉到了异状。

He places it in Lopera's hand.
赌局的落败者握住胜利之人的手，将那枚不光彩的骰子交还给它的主人。

Cruise Passenger: That's an interesting die you have there.
轮船游客：您有一枚非常有趣的骰子。

Lopera: It was only luck—
洛佩拉：这只是运——

Lopera: What's going on?
洛佩拉：怎么回事——

River Pirate: Do as I say! All of you!
河盗：都照我说的做！

Sotheby: Look, Vertin! There are some more "river cowboys" over there!
苏芙比：喔！他们是——是河上的“鳄鱼牛仔”吗？！

Vertin: I'm afraid they seem more like river pirates.
维尔汀：不，他们是强盗……

Vertin: Stay back, Sotheby!
维尔汀：苏芙比，退后！

Vertin: What do you want?
维尔汀：你想要什么？

River Pirate: Where's the captain? We're changing destinations. This ship will be docking in the favela now.
河盗：告诉船长，把船开到贫民区。

River Pirate: Or we'll blow it all to bits!
河盗：否则就等着大伙儿一起上天吧！

He flicks a lighter and lights the fuse of an explosive. He's panicked. Clearly, he's afraid of something.
他擦响打火机，在炸药引线边燎烧，发出威胁。他很慌张，在恐惧着什么。

Lopera: Get out of the way!!
洛佩拉：——都让开！！

Lopera has already drawn her guns.
比船长来得更快的，是已经拔枪的洛佩拉。

But the crowd is panicking. She can't get a clear shot.
但混乱的人群挡在前方，她没法扣动扳机。

Vertin: Hold your fire, Lopera!
维尔汀：洛佩拉，不能在这开枪！

Vertin: There are oil drums on board!
维尔汀：当心油桶！

Lopera notices the oil drums stacked behind the crowd. A single mistake could turn the whole ship into tomorrow's headline in the São Paulo papers.
洛佩拉这才注意到人群身后堆放着的油桶。稍有不慎，整艘船就会被炸成圣保罗明早的报纸头条。

Lopera: Ah, slimy rats.
洛佩拉：啧——耍花招的玩意儿。

She holsters her guns and charges forward with her bare fists.
于是她收起枪，徒手冲上去。

Lopera grapples with the thug before wrestling him to the ground and punching him repeatedly in the nose.
洛佩拉与暴徒扭打成一块儿，一拳一拳砸向对方鼻梁。

She tears the explosives off the pirate's body, but in that brief moment, the thug manages to pull one of her guns from its holster.
她扯下暴徒身上缠绕的炸药，却也因此被夺走了枪。

Vertin: Lopera!
维尔汀：洛佩拉！

The fight halts, the thug's trembling hand pointing the gun at Lopera. The oil drums are just a few steps away.
战斗忽然停了下来，暴徒的手颤抖着，将枪口指向洛佩拉——而她身后不远，就是油桶。

River Pirate: Either we go to the favela, or to the bottom of the river, clear? Make your call!
河盗：把船开到贫民区！否则一起去死！

Lopera: The favela ... Are you with the Apostles Brotherhood?
洛佩拉：贫民区……是天使兄弟会又回来了吗？

River Pirate: ...?!
河盗：……！

Lopera: They forced you into doing this, didn't they?
洛佩拉：他们胁迫了你，对不对？

Lopera: "Hijack the ship and bring it to us." Is that right?
洛佩拉：“去劫持今天的游轮，然后把船开过来。”

Lopera: Too bad for you. This ship won't be making any unplanned detours today.
洛佩拉：我能想到那些混账对你说了什么，但很遗憾，“短吻鳄”号今天的目的地是圣保罗。

The pirate's eyes widen as he freezes in place, only his legs trembling.
河盗瞪大眼，双腿颤抖着愣在原地。

Lopera hit the mark, but the thug's reaction is extreme.
洛佩拉说中了，但对方却做出了过激的反应。

His finger tightens on the trigger as his teeth chatter nervously.
那根骷髅般消瘦的手指摁住黑色扳机，牙齿打颤，在紧张中无意识地用力——

...
……

Lopera: Stupid!!!
洛佩拉：蠢货！！！

Lopera: Get down!
洛佩拉：趴下！

The bullet flies past her shoulder, instead piercing an oil drum behind her.
子弹没有击中她，而是打进了不稳定的金属油桶。

Lopera: Blaze-busters?
洛佩拉：爆轰弹？！

A blinding flash of light and a wave of heat hit them as the ship is quickly engulfed in flames.
首先是爆闪的火光，然后是响声和热风。附近的易燃物被爆炸接连引燃，火焰迅速蔓延。

The ship lurches violently as the blast tears through the vessel, and water starts flooding into the hull.
油桶引爆的冲击让船只剧烈摇晃，爆炸撕裂了船体，轮船开始解体。河水涌入船舱。

Passenger: Oh, my God!!!
游客：天哪！！！

Chef: Lifeboats! Get to the lifeboats now!
船厨：救生艇！找救生艇！

Passenger: God help us! This is hell!
游客：救救我……老天啊——！

Panic breaks out as the ship starts to split in half.
所有人都慌张地逃向甲板前端，轮船被拦腰炸断，向着被油桶炸裂开的位置倾斜、沉没。

More explosions follow.
爆炸接连传来冲击，头脑一片空白。

There's no sign of Ms. Kimberly in the crowd.
人群中没有看见金伯利的身影。

Lopera: Hold the railings! Stay on the deck!
洛佩拉：抓住栏杆！

Lopera shouts from the water as she clings to a piece of floating wood. She gasps for air as she bobs up and down in the water.
洛佩拉大口呛着河水，在水中起伏。她抱紧一块浮木，发来遥远的呼喊。

Chairs, benches, and sailing gear disappear as the ship sinks further and further into the depths of the river.
重力将所有人拽向河心的漩涡，更多的甲板被淹没了。

Sotheby clutches the guardrail tightly, but the ship is almost vertical and sinking fast.
苏芙比紧紧握住甲板护栏，但船只几乎要整个儿竖着沉下去了。

Vertin: Let go, Sotheby! You need to jump now! Join Lopera!
维尔汀：苏芙比！往洛佩拉那儿跳！

Screams fill the air as the tourists cling on for dear life, watching their friends and family hurtle toward the river.
落水的人们，悬空的人们。尖叫着的人们。在濒临死亡的险境中，眼前的一切都变得很远。

Like overripe oranges, one by one, they lose their grip and plunge into the depths.
像烂熟的橙子一样，游人松开被烧得滚烫的支撑物，掉进河里。

Sotheby: Vertin!
苏芙比：维尔汀——！

Flames lick the guardrails, melting the ship's cheap materials. The explosions ring in her ears.
火焰舔过金属护栏，融化了船上的劣质建材。爆炸引起耳鸣。

Sotheby closes her eyes and flings herself toward Lopera.
苏芙比闭上眼睛，跳向朝她游来的洛佩拉，落入水中。

Lucky for her, Lopera quickly grabs her hand and pulls her onto the wooden plank.
但她很幸运，同伴迅速抓住她的手，将她搂向自己那块木板。

Sotheby: Somebody, please get her! She's still ...
苏芙比：快去救维尔汀！！她……

"She" is still on the ship.
“她”还在船上。

With one final explosion, the ship is obliterated entirely.
轮船在最后一声爆炸中彻底解体。

The river swallows everything.
河水淹没了眼前的一切。

Vertin: ...
维尔汀：……

The sunlight ripples on the surface, growing farther and farther away.
金色的阳光在水面摇荡，变得越来越远……

Whose hand is that?
谁的手？

???: Can't have you dying here.
？？？：你可不能死在这儿……

???: *sigh* Why do we keep meeting in these awkward situations?
？？？：为什么呢，一遇到你总会发生这样的事情……

???: Consider my debt paid.
？？？：——可可点心棒，还给你了。

TO BE CONTINUED…
TO BE CONTINUED…

09  红腹锯脂鲤群 / The Red-Bellied Piranhas

So, things went sideways. That's what happens when you put guns in the hands of amateurs.
？？？：“金手指”扣下了扳机。产生了远超预料的结果，非专业人士就是这样。

But you handled the water better this time. You controlled your breathing, kept your airways clear, and this time you remembered to lie back and float.
？？？：降低心率、收缩喉管、关闭气道，与上一次溺水时相比，这次你做得不错，至少没那么狼狈了。

选项 / Choices

1. Who are you?
1. 你又是哪位？

Does it matter? I'm all you've got. We're at war—bullets flying, wounds festering, typhoid, cholera, and all the worst of it. It's a living hell, and only fools think there's any honor in it.
？？？：我是谁不重要。设想我们身处于硝烟滚滚的边境，后方的军营里没有心理医生，军官们大声地谈论着伤寒、霍乱、疟疾和肝炎，只有傻瓜才会将开肠破肚视为荣誉的象征。

You never expect to see a familiar face again, not in times like these.
？？？：这种场合的人员变动总是很快，你不能奢望来做报告的总是同一个人。

Now I need you to listen. There's something you should know.
？？？：现在请听我说，有一件事需要汇报。

选项 / Choices

1. What things?
1. 什么事？

The raid on the river was a ploy, and its mastermind is an old acquaintance. As for the ones who carried it out—you already heard about them this morning.
？？？：这次爆炸的背后藏着一场组织化的阴谋，档案袋的正面和反面分别写下了一个组织的名字。一个是你的老对头；而另一个你今早才有所耳闻。

They promised their followers they'd push south, and they did. With their supplies secured by a mysterious third party, they paraded through every town along the way, playing as "liberators" and taking out anyone they decided were their enemies.
？？？：他们许诺了会向南去，于是便能靠着第三方的物资耀武扬威，把自己比作救世主，用暴力铲除异己，既无组织也无行动纲领，可笑。

They swelled in numbers, larger than any local gang or cult had ever gotten before.
？？？：而更可笑的是他们借此收获了比以往更多追随者，一般的黑帮与宗教远不能及。

Now they swarm across the land like a school of red-bellied piranhas. Always hungry, ready to tear apart anything that crosses their path.
？？？：现在他们就像是一批红腹锯脂鲤群，当地人称呼这种鱼为“食人鱼”。在河里就能闻到血的味道，给每艘船都咬出几个洞。

选项 / Choices

1. What happened to the others?
1. 其他人呢？

Chances are they're doing fine, just like you.
？？？：既然连你都没事，你是否可以乐观地推断其他人都度过了这次危机？

Would've been nice if you caught what that little executioner and that "gold-hearted" gambler were talking about during their dice game. Guess you'll have to ask her later.
？？？：遗憾的是，你没能听清小行刑官跟乐善好施的游客在骰子游戏之余的闲谈。你可以在稍后再质问她。

选项 / Choices

1. Why don't *you* tell me instead?
1. 正面回答我

Listen, I'm not here to run your errands or play your messenger boy, so ask nicely.
？？？：我不是你的传令官，也不是你的亲兵，我也不想要这两个头衔，所以——

There's nothing more I need to tell you. For now.
？？？：目前来说，我没有义务继续向你汇报。

The Red-Bellied Piranhas
红腹锯脂鲤群

10  收监记录 / Incarceration Record

Special Incarceration Record
特别俘虏收监记录

Prisoner Name: Ms. Kimberly

Detention Process: During the raid on the Apostles Brotherhood stronghold, the subject was found alone, gnawing on a turkey leg, and was apprehended by Lieutenant Lopera's team. After interrogation, it was confirmed that the subject was indeed a follower of Manus Vindictae, but she knew even less than a typical follower, did not participate in the local rebellion, and was not involved in General Carlos's assassination. Therefore, unlike other deserters, she will not be executed but held in custody, awaiting Admiral Igor's decision.

Just leave her be for now.
单位名：金伯利女郎
收监经过：部队在突袭天使兄弟会据点时发现对象正在独自啃食火鸡腿，被洛佩拉少尉带队亲自拿下。后经过审问盘查，确认对象是跟随重塑之手前来的随行人员，对重塑之手的了解甚至少于一般追随者，且并未参与当地叛乱，与卡洛斯将军遇刺一事无关。故与其他逃兵区别处理，不执行死刑，作收监处置，等待伊戈尔将军发落。

放着不管。

11  杰出演员 / Tender Pretender

Tender Pretender
杰出演员

Riverbank
河岸

Galeno: The Brotherhood just took a beating from Zeno. If they're caught up with their own trouble, then we might have a golden opportunity to turn the people against them.
加莱亚诺：前阵子兄弟会被芝诺的人揍了一顿，老兄，我觉得这是个机会。他们现在全散了。

Galeno: We can't lose anyone else to their lies.
加莱亚诺：不能再让更多的人被他们骗走。

The old man next to him casts his fishing line. The bobber floats quietly on the river's surface. Across the river, a water anaconda eyes the two men beneath the shadow of a snakewood tree.
老人甩出鱼钩，浮标静静漂在河面。他在等待鱼饵咬钩。
河对岸，水蚺盘绕在蛇纹木下，窥伺两人。

Galeno: What do you say? Wanna talk about plans?
加莱亚诺：你怎么看？商量商量行动？

The old man doesn't speak. Instead, he removes his sunglasses and pushes a cup toward his friend.
老人没有言语，而是摘下墨镜，把杯子推给朋友。

???: Here. Drink up.
？？？：先来一杯。

Galeno: Thanks. Listen, as much as I enjoy being out here all day ... We've got to take action now. The sooner, the better, don't you think?
加莱亚诺：我不介意多来几杯。但是朋友，这是什么意思啊？

The young man takes the glass and downs it in one gulp, as his friend suggested.
年轻人接过酒杯，照着朋友的话一口喝光了酒。

???: Haha, hold your horses, young Galeno!
？？？：加莱亚诺呀！不要着急，哈哈！

???: Let me explain—say, I throw a punch at you now, really hit you good, what next?
？？？：如果我现在给你一拳，你可能会疼个好几天。

???: Maybe you'll storm off at first, but then, you round up your cousins, maybe grab that Tommy gun you borrowed from Colonel Tiago.
？？？：然后，你就会叫上几个表兄，提着从迭戈上校那儿租的汤普森冲锋枪，敲开我家的门。

Mr. Duncan: Then you'll find me, all alone, and say, "You're done, you old fart!"
邓肯先生：“你完了，混账邓肯！”

Mr. Duncan: !@#$%^%^&*...
邓肯先生：突突突——

The old man laughs, mimicking shooting with his hands.
老人笑起来，两只手比划着枪支射击的动作。

Mr. Duncan: Hahahaha! I'm not gonna harm you, kid. And if I ever do, remember that old Duncan must have his reasons and he'd never harm a friend intentionally.
邓肯先生：如果我真的揍了你，请记住，老邓肯永远忠于他的朋友，他一定有不得不这么做的原因。

Galeno: What is all that supposed to mean?
加莱亚诺：这话什么意思？

Mr. Duncan: Ha, I'm rambling. I mean, true enough that Lopera hit them hard, and we both know that the Brotherhood will aim to settle that score, and they won't wait long. But if we don't play this smart, it'll only cause them to lash out more.
邓肯先生：洛佩拉带人“揍了他们一拳”，现在兄弟会一定还在疼呢。但小心点，等他们缓过来，贫民区还会有麻烦。

Mr. Duncan: Still, we do have to consider one thing: those who chose to follow them never come back.
邓肯先生：被兄弟会骗走和掳走的年轻人，你见过哪个回来的吗？

Galeno: No, they don't.
加莱亚诺：没有、一个也没有……连是生是死都不知道。

Mr. Duncan: So, let's seize the day, grab opportunity by the horns, and maybe we stop losing more of our friends to those bastards. Right now, seems to me that Zeno is our best bet.
邓肯先生：带剩下的人加入芝诺吧，他们能对付兄弟会。如果要乘凉，大伙儿就该找一棵靠得住的大树。

The young man considers this proposition.
年轻人思考片刻，认同了他的看法。

Galeno: That just might work, old man. With the Brotherhood falling apart, Zeno will start to look pretty good to folks here.
加莱亚诺：不错，兄弟会现在快完蛋了，去芝诺当兵，是个好想法。

Mr. Duncan: Ah, but not so fast, kid! We need a little patience. Let the line out a little first.
邓肯先生：但现在别着急，年轻人，你看，现在河上静得连片叶子都没有。

Mr. Duncan: If you fail to prepare, you're prepared to fail.
邓肯先生：钓鱼之前，咱们得先预备好装鱼的塑料袋。

The bobber twitches.
红色浮漂动了动。

Mr. Duncan: Got it! Excellent!
邓肯先生：咬了！咬了——！

The old man jumps to his feet, reeling his fishing rod and fighting the unseen enemy in the water.
老人激动地站起身子，抓稳鱼竿，跟水里看不见的敌人搏斗。

Mr. Duncan: Aw! Don't think you could get away from me!
邓肯先生：这下你跑不掉了！

The fishing line plunges down into the river, the rod bending into a crescent.
鱼线直直扎下河面，老人的鱼竿已经扭成了一道圆满的月弧。

Mr. Duncan: Ah! You like to wrestle, huh? Alright, let's see who gets the upper hand!
邓肯先生：好啊，你要跟我比力气！看看我俩谁更有劲儿！

Mr. Duncan: Old Duncan is not about to lose a battle to—
邓肯先生：我这条老狗……可不会……输给……

Mr. Duncan: A fish! Hahahaha!
邓肯先生：年轻人呀！啊哈！

It's a giant puffpuff fish, larger than any he's seen before.
一只巨大的胖气球鱼，通体透明，比他从前见过的任何一只都更大。

Galeno: Wow! Do you see the size of its belly?! It's huge!
加莱亚诺：老天呀，它肚子鼓得这么大！

What looks like a large green cake is floating inside its translucent belly.
半透明的鱼肚子里，漂浮着一个大大的绿色小蛋糕。

Mr. Duncan: Haha, what a deal! Catch one, get one free! How generous, Mother River.
邓肯先生：哈哈，吃了一个小家伙的大鱼？这条河还能给咱们弄点什么怪东西？

Mr. Duncan: Alright, jokes aside, Galeno. Come on, help me get her out.
邓肯先生：圣母保佑，还好她让我俩遇到了。

Old Duncan pulls out a knife and slices open the fish's belly.
老邓肯抽出短刀，剖开鱼肚子。

His catch bursts like a balloon, releasing air, water, and … a girl.
他的渔获就像一个爆开的气球，流出空气、水和水中的女孩儿……

Galeno: This has got to be the strangest thing you've ever fished out from the river!
加莱亚诺：啧啧……除了鱼，你这老家伙真是什么都能钓到……

Another castaway floats downstream.
从上游又漂来另一位落难者。

Lopera: Duncan, Duncan!
洛佩拉：邓肯……！嘿！邓肯！

Across the river, the water anaconda locks onto its target, slipping into the water and swimming toward her.
静候在蛇纹木之下的捕猎者盯上了她，抢先滑入水中，朝落难者游近。

Galeno: Duncan, look! She's got an anaconda chasing after her!
加莱亚诺：邓肯，不对劲，有条蛇过去了！

The water anaconda zigzags toward its prey.
水蚺以“Z”字动作游向猎物。

Mr. Duncan: I saw it. Don't you worry.
邓肯先生：好呀，那儿还躲着个坏家伙。

Old Duncan sets the soaked girl down before lifting a blowpipe and aiming at the snake.
老邓肯放下湿淋淋的女孩，举起吹笛，对准目标——

*whoosh*
咻咻。

The dart strikes, quickly draining the anaconda of its strength. It sinks to the riverbed like a coil of old rope.
中箭的爬行动物迅速失去力气，像一团麻绳，慢慢沉下水底。

Mr. Duncan: Hang in there, Lopera! Come this way, we'll get you!
邓肯先生：洛佩拉！再坚持一会儿，朝这儿游！

Mr. Duncan: Here! Take it!
邓肯先生：接住——！

He grabs a nearby vine and throws it into the water. Lopera grabs it and pulls herself toward the shore.
他抛出一根荨麻藤，落水的友人抓住这根长藤，游向岸边。

Lopera: Thank goodness you were here to grab me.
洛佩拉：……多谢了，老伙计！

She clambers onto the shore, shakes the water from her hair, and sits leaning back on her hands, breathing heavily.
她踩着湿泥攀上岸，甩甩脑袋，双手撑地坐下，长长地舒了几口气。

Lopera: But what are you doing all the way out here?
洛佩拉：但你怎么……会在这儿？你不是在荣军院？

Mr. Duncan: I was wondering the same thing about you, girl. Seems like a strange place for a swim. I suppose you wouldn't know anything about the girl we just fished out over there?
邓肯先生：这话得我问你……你怎么从河上漂来的？还有她，鱼肚子里冒出个人，怎么回事？

He points to the little girl who rolled out of the fish's belly.
老邓肯指向从鱼肚子里滚出的小女孩。

Lopera: Sotheby!
洛佩拉：苏芙比！

She's still breathing. The air trapped in the fish's belly must have saved her life.
她有呼吸。鱼腹中储存的空气意外救了她一命。

Lopera crawls over to her companion and smacks her back, helping her spit out the water in her lungs.
洛佩拉爬向同伴，抱住她的后背，帮她倒出口腹中的积水。

Lopera: Hey, can you hear me?
洛佩拉：嘿，醒醒！

Mr. Duncan: Easy now, Lopera! We just cut her out of that fish's belly.
邓肯先生：轻点儿，洛佩拉。

Sotheby coughs out the water in her lungs as she regains consciousness.
苏芙比咳出河水，慢慢苏醒。

Mr. Duncan: You're a veritable Jonah, aren't you?
邓肯先生：上帝保佑呀，你运气不错，孩子，那条鱼肚子里没装满水。

Sotheby: Mm ... Hmm?
苏芙比：唔……嗯？

A familiar face appears in her blurred vision.
一张熟悉的脸，在水中模糊着，像透过雨幕看见的世界。

Sotheby: I-I dreamt of a very, very big fish.
苏芙比：我梦到一条大鱼，好大好大……

Sotheby: And I was in its belly. It was as wide as the sky. I couldn't breathe.
苏芙比：它的肚子变成了天空……呼吸很难受……

Sotheby: A terribly strange dream, Mr. Karson.
苏芙比：可是……卡森先生……？

Mr. Duncan: She's awake!
邓肯先生：她醒了！

Sotheby: Mr. Karson?!
苏芙比：卡森先生？！

This is him. The man from the photograph is standing before her. She freezes.
相框中的人来到了眼前。她愣住了。

Sotheby: You ... How ...
苏芙比：你……为什么……

The water slowly clears from her vision. The face before her is both familiar and strange.
模糊视野的积水一点点散开。靠近自己的这张脸变得陌生又熟悉。

She knows it, yet she doesn't.
熟悉却陌生。

Sotheby: There's a little dirt on me. Here, and on the collar, too. Oh, goodness.
苏芙比：这儿脏兮兮的……这儿……那儿……天哪……

Sotheby: Why am I here? I ... What ...
苏芙比：我怎么会……唔……这……

"Mr. Karson" kneels beside her. But he's wearing some very odd clothes.
“卡森管家”换了身衣服，出现在身旁，惊醒了她。

Sotheby: Could it really be you, Mr. Karson?
苏芙比：卡森先生！真的是你吗！

Mr. Duncan: Karson? You must be mistaken, little lady. My name is Duncan. I'm a friend of Lopera.
邓肯先生：卡森？你肯定认错了，小淑女，我不知道什么卡森先生。我是邓肯。你说对吧，洛佩拉？

Lopera: That's right. Sotheby, this is Duncan from the Veterans' Residence. We've known each other for a long time.
洛佩拉：苏芙比，这老家伙是邓肯，荣军院的邓肯，我认识他很久了。

Lopera: You must be confusing him with someone else.
洛佩拉：你认错人了。

Mr. Duncan: Now, up on your feet, little lady.
邓肯先生：先起来吧，孩子。

He takes her arm and pulls her out of the mud.
邓肯先生伸手握住她的胳膊，一把将小淑女提出河岸的软泥堆。

Sotheby: *gasp* Mr. Karson!
苏芙比：哇，吓了我一跳——！

Sotheby: Oh, sorry! It's just, I didn't expect you to suddenly pull my arm.
苏芙比：卡森先生……你刚才突然拽了我一下……

Mr. Duncan: Pardon me, little lady. We tend to be very straightforward here in the favela.
邓肯先生：原谅我，小姐，我就是个贫民区的老头子。做事就这么直接。

Sotheby: It's quite alright. Only—No, no, no, I just don't understand! You look exactly like Mr. Karson; how could you be anyone else?
苏芙比：不，不不不！我不明白，一个和卡森先生长得一样的人，为什么会不是卡森先生？

Sotheby: Except for your attire, and those shoes ...
苏芙比：但你确实……你的、你的——

Her butler would never dress like this, sockless with patched-up clothing.
不同的装扮。严肃的庄园管家绝不会穿成这样——脚上绝不会不穿袜子，衣服上绝不会出现补丁。

She notices the liquid swirling in his cup.
她看到杯子里摇晃的深色液体。

Sotheby: And the liquor?
苏芙比：酒……？

No, her butler would never drink in front of the lady of the house.
不，庄园管家绝不会带着含有酒精或兴奋水的饮料来见他的大小姐。

Sotheby decides to take a bold guess. She jumps up and nervously grabs Mr. Duncan's shirt.
她决定再做一个大胆的猜测——小淑女跳起来，紧张地伸手握住邓肯先生的衬衣。

Mr. Duncan: Oh, pardon me, little miss. Are you not comfortable with us drinking? Galeno, put the cup away for me, please.
邓肯先生：你生气了吗，孩子？也许你不喜欢看到老人喝酒，加莱亚诺，帮我拿下杯子——

Sotheby: No, I only meant ... Mr. Karson, it truly seems as though you've forgotten everything! Oh, but I'm being terribly unladylike.
苏芙比：这、这样很不淑女！卡森先生！你都忘了吗！《淑女的原则》，我、我违背了……！

She rambles incoherently.
她语无伦次。

This behavior is totally against The Lady's Code of Conduct, yet "Mr. Karson" remains utterly unfazed.
粗鲁的行为，不符合《淑女的原则》，但对方却不为所动。

Sotheby: Still, you-you don't seem to mind at all.
苏芙比：但是……但是……卡森先生……邓肯先生……你……

Growing increasingly confused, Sotheby lowers her head, still clutching the hem of "Mr. Karson's" shirt.
她收到的只是对方无尽的困惑。最终，她泄气了，低下头，双手却还挂在“卡森”先生的衬衣衣角上。

Mr. Duncan: I must be a dead-ringer for this guy to have gotten you all flustered, little miss. Seems like you cared very deeply for this Mr. Karson?
邓肯先生：听起来，他一定是一位对你来说特别重要的人。他是不是和我长得很像？

Sotheby: Yes, I did. So, then, you really aren't him?
苏芙比：你们……真的不是同一个人吗？

Mr. Duncan: Sorry to disappoint, little lady. But I'm flattered by the resemblance. Maybe I can be your friend too. In fact, feel free to call me Karson—why not? A rose by any other name, eh?
邓肯先生：哈哈，没关系，如果我跟你的这位朋友很像。你可以把我当作他，我愿意为你扮演他。

The old man hooks his pinky with Sotheby's and presses his thumb firmly against hers.
老人与她拉钩约定。

Sotheby: Mr. Karson ...
苏芙比：卡森先生……

Mr. Duncan: I know how hard it can be. I lost my wife, and my comrade-in-arms. I even managed to lose his daughter, after I promised to take care of her.
邓肯先生：我理解失去的感受，我失去了爱人，也失去了已故战友嘱咐我照顾的女儿。

Mr. Duncan: She'd have been just about your age.
邓肯先生：她要是还健在，也该跟你一个年纪了，孩子。

Mr. Duncan: I'm rambling again. I suppose what I'm saying is, I know what it feels like to lose someone you love.
邓肯先生：心中有一位想见却永远见不到的人，我理解这种痛苦，孩子。

She looks lost, unsure of what to think, but she still holds onto the old man's hand, seemingly making a small promise with him.
她怅惘着，不知道怎么样才是正确的。但她仍然抵住老人的手指，似乎和他达成了一个小小的约定。

Looking around, Sotheby realizes someone important is missing.
现在，环顾四周，苏芙比发现少了一位重要的伙伴。

Sotheby: Oh, no. But what about Ms. Vertin! We've lost her! Where could she be now? We must find her!
苏芙比：呀，糟糕了。维尔汀、我和维尔汀走丢了……我们得把她救回来！

Lopera: Slow down. We need to think things through first. Those knuckleheads blew up the entire ship.
洛佩拉：先别着急，我们得想想办法。那该死的混蛋引爆了油桶，船沉了。

Lopera: Which means our comms belong to the caimans now.
洛佩拉：通讯设备也都坏了。

Everything's in disarray. There's no way to contact the Timekeeper. They don't even know if she's alive.
一场意外把事情搅得一团糟。无法联络上司辰，甚至不知道她的生死。

Mr. Duncan: Not to worry. I've been through worse! Back in '82, on the Rio Negro ... Oh, before I go rambling on again ...
邓肯先生：别担心，八二年我在内格罗河迷路的时候，情况比这糟得多。

Mr. Duncan: Let's head back to the city! Come on now. Trust good old Duncan.
邓肯先生：回“城”里去吧，相信邓肯！邓肯总有办法！

Mr. Duncan: Sorry, I mean Karson! Trust Karson to lead the way!
邓肯先生：噢……不对，现在应该是卡森。很好，相信卡森吧！

TO BE CONTINUED…
TO BE CONTINUED…

12  无端指控 / False Accusations

False Accusations
无端指控

Chapel of Our Lady of Sorrows
悲伤圣母教堂

A man in black lifts a fallen statue.
穿着黑衣的先生扶起圣像。

???: I've never wanted to put São Paulo through this chaos.
？？？：我从未想过让圣保罗陷入混乱。

He shields the faint flame in his hand, reigniting the candles one by one.
他护着掌心微弱的火花，将白蜡烛一盏盏重新点燃。

???: Order, peace, unity—those are my purposes.
？？？：秩序，和平，服从。

???: Much has been wagered here, Marcando, much. Now, it has been lost.
？？？：马坎多，他们在这儿做了什么？

Marcando: Your Reverence, it was Zeno. Those animals! We'll make them pay for what they did!
马坎多：芝诺的人干的。该死，绝不能就这么算了！

Marcando: Is that why you've decided to grace us with your presence, Your Reverence?
马坎多：桑托斯阁下，恕我冒昧，我猜您一定也是为了这件事才来的。

Marcando: There is a lot to do if we're going to recover the ground, we—I've lost, Your Reverence. And there's something more, a new doctor down at the Veterans' Residence.
马坎多：我们的人被打散了不少，最近还来了个荣军院的医生。

Marcando: Some blind woman. I would not bother you with it, Lord Santos. Except that she's been helping people in the favela and it is swaying them towards Zeno.
马坎多：那女人是个瞎子，但她给很多人看病，替荣军院和芝诺笼络这儿的人。

Santos: Then this doctor is with Zeno?
桑托斯：她是芝诺的人吗？

Marcando: She must be. She's always at the Veterans' Residence—spends most of the day with those old war dogs.
马坎多：一定是的。她一直驻扎在荣军院里，和那群老混蛋亲近得很。

His expression is tense as he mops the sweat from his forehead.
马坎多表情紧张，不断擦拭着额头的汗水。

Santos: A doctor gaining the trust of the people in the favela? She'd make quite the prize, wouldn't you say, Marcando?
桑托斯：一位在贫民区很有影响力的军医，医术精湛，深受信赖。

Santos: A much more valuable bargaining chip to Zeno than a few deserters.
桑托斯：我想，她对芝诺的价值远大于几个被捕的逃兵。

Marcando understands perfectly. In São Paulo, kidnappings are as common as bread.
马坎多明白了头领的意思——在圣保罗，绑架形同家常便饭。

Santos: You will see this task through, Marcando. But before that ...
桑托斯：这件事交给你去办，但在那之前……

Santos: We need to pay a visit to the favela. I've brought us some new blood.
桑托斯：马坎多，叫上我们最忠诚的朋友，去贫民区——我带来了新的力量。

Favela
河岸贫民区

Mr. Duncan: See? Just like this. Wrap the wire around it—better do it twice—and align the red and black, slowly.
邓肯先生：就这样，看见了吗，把线缠两圈，然后红色的跟黑色的对齐，慢慢接上……

He delicately connects the copper wires and the current buzzes to life.
老邓肯将铜线接在一块儿，电流“嗞——嗞——”发出声响。

Sotheby: Unbelievable!
苏芙比：这真是不可思议……！

Mr. Duncan: It's science, kids. Nothing unbelievable about it.
邓肯先生：这就是科学，小子们。学会了吗？科学的力量！

All fixed, he hands the communicator back to Lopera.
他将修好的联络器还给洛佩拉。

Lopera: I don't know how to thank you, Duncan!
洛佩拉：帮大忙了，老家伙，还是你最可靠呀！

She straightens the antenna, adjusts the channel, and hurriedly contacts the base to report the situation.
炼金师掰直天线，调试频道，联络基地汇报情况。

Igor's Office
伊戈尔办公室

Moldir: Admiral, Lopera just called in.
莫莉德尔：洛佩拉来的联络。

Admiral Igor: Put her through.
伊戈尔将军：接。

Moldir: Lopera, it's me. What happened?
莫莉德尔：洛佩拉，是我。发生什么了？

Lopera: Our ship was sunk. Ms. Sotheby and I washed ashore near the favelas.
洛佩拉：游轮炸了，是意外。我和苏芙比漂到了贫民区。

Lopera: As for the Timekeeper, she went overboard. We lost sight of her in the commotion. Her current status is unknown.
洛佩拉：司辰……很糟糕，我不知道她去哪了，但愿没什么事。

Moldir: ...?!
莫莉德尔：……！

The admiral remains expressionless. He raises a finger, signaling Moldir to inquire further.
将军面无表情，抬起手指，示意副官女士追问情况。

Moldir: We'll send people to find her and escort her to the Veterans' Residence.
莫莉德尔：基地会立刻派人去搜救司辰，这件事交给我们。找到司辰后，她会被护送到荣军院。

Lopera: Additionally, Lieutenant, I have some fresh recruits eager to enroll with Zeno. But they will need the admiral's assurances that their families will be under our protection.
洛佩拉：这儿的贫民区的年轻人想加入芝诺，他们害怕被报复，需要将军的承诺。

Moldir looks at the admiral.
副官女士看向老将军。

He nods in agreement.
将军点点头。他答应了。

Moldir: They have it. What's their ETA?
莫莉德尔：父亲非常欢迎他们，芝诺很需要这样热情的年轻人。他们什么时候来？

Lopera: They'll leave at once.
洛佩拉：他们会立即出发。

Moldir: Pera ...
莫莉德尔：洛佩——

Moldir: *sigh* That little hothead.
莫莉德尔：……唉，总是这么急。

Moldir: Sir, your orders?
莫莉德尔：父亲，现在怎么办？

Admiral Igor: Leave the matter to me, Moldir. I will send someone to take care of this.
伊戈尔将军：司辰的事我会安排。

Moldir falls silent as she looks at her aging father, concern in her eyes.
副官女士不再追问，担忧地看着年迈的父亲。

Silence spreads between father and daughter as the admiral focuses on the dice in his hand.
老将军紧盯着手中的骰子——沉默在父女间无声地蔓延着。

Galeno: What did the admiral say?
加莱亚诺：将军阁下怎么说？

Lopera: He agreed!
洛佩拉：他答应了！

Galeno: Did you hear that? Zeno will protect us!
加莱亚诺：好极了！

Good news indeed. The young man claps his hands in excitement.
一个值得庆祝的好消息，让年轻人兴奋地拍起手。

But just as the celebration starts, it is cut abruptly short.
但人群中传来一阵响动。有人来了。

Lopera: I have no doubt. They were working for the Brotherhood.
洛佩拉：果然是他们……船上的猜测是对的。

Mr. Duncan: The Apostles Brotherhood? They've bounced back quicker than I'd hoped.
邓肯先生：啊，天使兄弟会，我还以为他们完蛋了……

Mr. Duncan: Still, we'll need to be cautious. Figure out just what they're after.
邓肯先生：看看他们要干嘛。

Lopera: That guy looks familiar.
洛佩拉：那家伙有点儿眼熟……

It's the stranger she met on the ship—he's walking among the Brotherhood.
她看清了走在兄弟会队伍中的那位先生——是那位曾在船上遭遇的陌生人。

Lopera: That's him! He was on the ship with me.
洛佩拉：……该死的，这不是那个在船上的家伙吗？

He walks toward the crowd, passing out food and other useful items to those he passes.
一位穿黑衣的先生走向人群，沿途施舍向他乞讨的穷人。

Santos: The Brotherhood has experienced a setback, yes—as all great causes do! But we will rise again, for we serve the one true purpose.
桑托斯：兄弟会遭遇了小小的失败，但没关系。我们会重新强壮起来。

Santos: Hear me now, brothers and sisters, fellow arcanists!
桑托斯：同胞们，饱受欺压的神秘学家同胞们——

Santos: Our days of anguish and deprivation are over. The Sufferer will walk among us once more, and we will bask in THEIR divine grace.
桑托斯：“受苦者”将从尘土中复苏。祂将再次行走在大地，为祂走过的每一寸土地祝福。

Santos: The day of triumph draws near. Soon, our wagers will pay off. All will reap what we have sown! You only need stand with us.
桑托斯：我们的日子就要到了，跟随我来——

Santos: And take THEIR blessings. In return for this, THEY ask only for your unwavering allegiance.
桑托斯：以“受苦者”的名义——我为祂施舍，要求你们的忠诚。

He pulls paper bills from his suitcase and throws them into the crowd.
黑色手提箱里撒出纸钞，像雪片般落到船上、水上。

Yet the crowd remains still in their confusion.
但迷茫的人群愣在原地，一动不动。

Santos: My brothers and sisters. Speak. What do you seek?
桑托斯：朋友们，告诉我，你们需要什么？

No one answers him.
没有人回答他。

Resident of the Favela: Sir, please, we only want to see our family again.
贫民区住民：阁下，我们想见到自己的亲人……

Resident of the Favela: Since they've joined the Brotherhood, we've had no word. We haven't seen or heard from them at all.
贫民区住民：从前跟您离开的那些人，加入了天使兄弟会的人。

Nothing is more important than family.
没什么比家人更重要。

Santos: Ah, but you will see them again soon. I tell you that they are even now faithfully serving our kind in a new land of prosperity.
桑托斯：我保证你们会和家人团聚，他们正在一片繁荣的土地上供奉己身。

Santos: They're in Ushuaia, in Antarctica, in all places where the past and the future converge.
桑托斯：在乌斯怀亚，在南极，在流淌着往日与未来的交汇之地。

Santos: There will be no more poverty! No more hatred! No more chaos! We shall reclaim what's rightfully ours!
桑托斯：不再有人类带来的苦难——贫穷、仇杀与混乱，他们窃据高位，掠夺了属于我们的生活。

Santos: Remember this: Humans are vile and sinful creatures. They tortured our men and women, leaving our children to cry in hunger and desperation.
桑托斯：我们终日的劳苦与怨愤，我们怀中婴儿的哭喊与饥饿，哪一个不是人类种下的恶果？

Santos: But they've already played their hand, all their reason, their science, and their despicable organizations. They raised these rotten edifices to weaken us and leech off of our misfortunes, to blind us from our true purpose.
桑托斯：理性与秩序，科学与组织——他们筑起高墙，却将我们排除在外，用我们的不幸饲喂他们不知餍足的暴欲。

Santos: But no longer! Soon, their games will be over, and every injustice and oppression they've held over our people will be returned tenfold! All that they've built will crumble down over their heads!
桑托斯：神秘学家的每一滴汗水与眼泪，我们生而遭受的不公与欺压，都将在祂的荣耀中一扫而空。

The crowd whispers in hushed tones, afraid to speak any louder.
听众中，众人窃语着，不敢出声。

Hiding nearby, Duncan quietly leans over to Lopera and whispers a few words before handing her a rabbit patch.
邓肯先生凑近洛佩拉耳边，低声说了几句话，又把自己的一枚兔子补丁交给她。

Santos: Tell me, my fellow brothers and sisters, what is evil?
桑托斯：请告诉我，同胞们，什么是恶？

No one answers. The murmurs of the crowd quickly fade, creating a heady silence. A few crows fly low overhead.
没人回答他，人群中的窃语收敛了，四下一片寂静。鸦群飞过低空。

Santos: Evil is the absence of good.
桑托斯：恶是非善。

Santos: And to find what is good ...
桑托斯：而善……

Santos: ... is to follow our path!
桑托斯：就是跟从！跟从我们的道路！

Santos: The High One has promised us strength and glory!
桑托斯：祂已经给予了奖赏！力量与荣光！

Two followers carry a box, emanating a sinister aura over to him.
随即，追随者们顺应呼喊，将一个散发着不祥气息的箱子拽向他身边。

He opens it, revealing the blessings of the Sufferer for all to see.
漆黑的盲目与罪恶在日光下显现，将受苦者的赐福展示予众人。

Santos: Come with me!
桑托斯：同胞们，跟从我！

He throws open his arms as he calls out once more, taking a step back in humble reverence to the Sufferer's glory.
他再一次高呼，张开双臂，退后一步，对受苦者的荣耀极尽谦卑。

Sotheby: That's a Manus's mask!
苏芙比：天哪！重塑之手！

In that nightmare back in 1929, disaster struck because of the people who wore these masks. The memory is still vivid in Sotheby's mind.
苏芙比见过那张面具。在噩梦般的1929年，灾厄就因为散布面具的人们而来。

Santos: Sorrow will weaken you, and fear may seize your hearts!
桑托斯：我理解你们的悲伤和疑惧。

Santos: Instead, let faith be your guide! Those who take that leap shall find themselves lifted up upon the Apostles' wings.
桑托斯：不要动摇，同胞们，切勿怀疑。舍身跳下的，必有翅膀接住你们。

Santos: While those who hesitate will be scorched by the fires at their feet.
桑托斯：畏缩躲逃的，必被天上的硫磺与地上的刀刃杀死。

Santos: I've said all that must be said. We'll meet again here, in three days' time.
桑托斯：你们有三天时间考虑。

He leaves them with a "gracious and generous" deadline.
他留下了“宽容而慷慨”的时限。

Sotheby: Don't listen to him! He's a lunatic!
苏芙比：不！他是个坏蛋！

The little lady bravely steps forward, fervently warning the crowd.
小淑女勇敢地站出来，气愤地，向在场众人发出警告。

Marcando: And just who are you?
马坎多：你他妈是哪来的小鬼？

Santos: Marcando.
桑托斯：马坎多。

Santos quickly stops his subordinate.
桑托斯拦住手下。

Santos: As students of the Truth, we have been granted a much stronger weapon than violence.
桑托斯：真理比刀剑更有力量。我们既然掌握真理，就不要用刀剑惊吓朋友。

Mr. Duncan: Lopera, follow my lead.
邓肯先生：洛佩拉，记住，照我说的做。

He stands beside Sotheby, taking her hand in his.
苏芙比被恶狼的目光注视着，邓肯先生握住她的手，也跟着站起来。

Mr. Duncan: Sir, please forgive her. She has mistaken you for those pirates on the Tietê River.
邓肯先生：阁下，请原谅，她把你们错认成了铁特河上的恶棍。

Santos: So, just a misunderstanding, then? I'm no lunatic, after all?
桑托斯：那么，这就是个美丽的误会了，是吗？

Mr. Duncan: Yes, Your Reverence. She came across a terrible raid on her way here, you see. The poor girl was left terrified.
邓肯先生：是的，这孩子来的时候被他们的人抢劫了，她很害怕。

Santos: Well, I'll need more than a few words to convince my friends we have not been insulted.
桑托斯：我要怎么相信你的话呢，老先生？

Loyalty without tribute cannot be trusted.
没有献纳的效忠不值得信任。

Mr. Duncan: I-I'm an old man with nothing to give you but the truth! Believe me, sir! I-I came all this way just for you! I want to join the Brotherhood!
邓肯先生：因为……我是为了投靠您而来！为了加入天使兄弟会而来！

His words surprise everyone. Those who know Duncan are shocked, and those who don't are confused.
他的话让在场众人都惊讶地嘘出了声——认识老邓肯的人惊异，陌生他的人则感到困惑。

Mr. Duncan: She didn't mean to offend any of the gentlemen here! Please, sir, she's only a kid.
邓肯先生：看在同胞的面子上，阁下，请原谅孩子小小的冒犯。

Santos: Of course. I try never to take offense at a sweet young lady. However, if I'm to believe you, I will need you to up your ante to prove you are worthy, sir.
桑托斯：我不会对孩子生气。但是你，老先生，证明你的忠诚。

The old man kneels, just as he did when he played the role of "Karson" for Sotheby.
老人虔敬地屈膝下跪——如同他为小淑女扮演“卡森”。

Mr. Duncan: If that's what you need, Lord, allow me to sweeten the deal. See that guy over there? His name is Galeno. He's been recruiting for Zeno. He told me so himself!
邓肯先生：那边有个叫加莱亚诺的小子，他在策划把这儿的年轻人带到芝诺去当兵！他亲口跟我说的！

He turns and points at Galeno in the crowd.
他转身，指向人群中的加莱亚诺。

Galeno: Duncan?
加莱亚诺：……邓肯！

Santos: Bring the young man closer, Marcando.
桑托斯：马坎多，带他过来。

Galeno: Oof! Are you out of your mind? Don't touch me!
加莱亚诺：该死的……我才不会让你们……！

He tries to flee, but the thugs rush at him on command.
年轻人惊怒之下，试图抽身逃离，但暴徒已经在号令下向他冲去——

In the chaos of the battle, Lopera grabs Sotheby, and they hop onto a small wooden boat.
战斗引发了骚乱，洛佩拉趁乱抓住苏芙比。一艘木划艇将两人带离风暴中心。

Lopera: Let's go!
洛佩拉：快走！

Surrounded, Galeno finally stops his struggle when a knife is pressed to his throat.
另一方面，年轻人做着徒劳的反抗，直到一把匕首抵住喉咙。

Santos: I admire your courage, but bravery alone won't protect you from harm. Tell me, son, is what he said the truth of it?
桑托斯：你很勇敢。但现在我决定割开你的喉咙。告诉我，他说的是真的吗？

Galeno: ...
加莱亚诺：……

He turns his head, refusing to respond.
年轻人倔强地扭过头，拒绝答复。

Santos: Now, you'll give me the names of Zeno's new recruits. Who are they?
桑托斯：哪些人会跟你去投奔芝诺？

A drop of blood falls from Galeno's neck as the cold blade of the knife presses into his skin.
一滴血从年轻人的喉咙处滴下。匕首冰冷的锋刃舔弄着他的脖子。

Galeno: Everyone! Every man and woman in this city that still has a shred of integrity!
加莱亚诺：所有人！每个有骨气的人都想！

Galeno: Ah!
加莱亚诺：呃啊——！

Duncan punches Galeno squarely in the face, sending him reeling.
“叛徒”邓肯对着年轻人的脸，狠狠揍了一拳，将他仰头击倒。

Mr. Duncan: "Hahahaha! I'm not gonna harm you, kid. And if I ever do, remember that old Duncan must have his reasons and he'd never harm a friend intentionally."
邓肯先生：“如果我真的揍了你，请记住，老邓肯永远忠于他的朋友，他一定有不得不这么做的原因。”

Galeno looks at him. He has that same old look on his face—determined, brave, and resolute.
他看向自己的老朋友——那是他熟悉的眼神，坚毅而勇敢。

Santos: This young man has one day to think it through. He will give us his names.
桑托斯：这位年轻人有一天时间考虑，让他交出名单——所有想加入芝诺的人的名单。

He sheathes his knife and hands Galeno over to his new companion.
他收起匕首，将年轻人交给新的伙伴。

Santos: Or, he will give us his eyes and tongue as offerings. Mr. Duncan, let this task be set on you. Prove yourself worthy of our trust.
桑托斯：明天中午我要见到那些名字，或者……见到他的舌头跟眼睛。老先生，你来动手。

Mr. Duncan: It will be my honor, Your Reverence.
邓肯先生：如您所愿，阁下。

Santos: Marcando, you'll stay with them. Don't forget what I told you earlier.
桑托斯：马坎多，你也留下来。别忘了你的任务。

TO BE CONTINUED…
TO BE CONTINUED…

13  自救与拯救 / Who is the Savior?

This place was once known for its inexhaustible mines, the riches of its rainforests, and its brave and passionate people. But now, the first two are gone.
？？？：这个美丽的地方曾经享有着号称永不枯竭的矿产、大地之肺的雨林与热情勇敢的人民，而现在他们只剩下了第三者。

Chaos and power struggles loom over the younger generation, while the rich and well-connected sit atop their piles of corpses and rubble and feast. Dining off golden plates with silver spoons, turning their noses up from the dogs at their feet.
？？？：混乱和割据夺走了年轻人的美梦，而既得利益者总以为自己仍旧坐在巨大的金山上，殊不知那下面全是烂头骨、彩色瓦砾和携带病菌的老鼠。

But that gold has lost its shine and their silver spoons have tarnished, everyone can see them for what they really are. So, what now? Either the people save themselves or turn their fate over to the hands of the so-called "savior." What would you do, if it were you?
？？？：镀金的遮羞布已然脱落，内里的草絮正朝所有人诉说着贫瘠与困窘，现在人们面前有两条路——自救，和加入所谓的“拯救”。我就此来咨询你的意见。

选项 / Choices

1. I'd save myself first.
1. 先自救，再救人

2. So, why can't this "savior" save me?
2. “拯救”不能救自己么？

分支 1 / Branch 1

That's a smart choice. Now that the carnival's over, people have to deal with more than just their hangovers—they must figure out how to save their own skins.
？？？：保全自己，很明智。狂欢的宿醉后，异常虚软无力的身躯发出警告。每个人都不得不抬起被橡木塞堵满的浮肿头颅，拼了命地想一个自救的法子。

Most though will just run around like chickens with their heads cut off, begging for their pain to be taken away. But all they'll get is another morphine shot—nothing close to a real solution.
？？？：于是人们开始盲目奔走。粗大的针管扎进了他们的心脏，他们以为那是一管强心剂，实际上是盐酸和吗啡。

Fear spreads like a disease. Until an entire generation is out on the streets, hoping to find anything that will lift them out of their misery.
？？？：当某个倒霉蛋因生计而累垮时，名为迷茫与躁动的瘟疫开始传播。时至今日，流氓与赤贫者四处可见，结果没有人能救下这一带，以及这整一代人。

分支 2 / Branch 2

Who's asking the questions here, me or you? But fine, I'll clue you in a little about this "savior".
？？？：请不要用问题回答问题，这不符合交流守则。但这次我会回答你。

I once met a kid from Buenos Aires who told me about the "hand of god" that gave them the World Cup. He saw it as proof there was a higher power, something men should follow and seek protection from.
？？？：我曾听一个来自布宜诺斯艾利斯的孩子谈起“上帝之手”。他说那是光荣的行径，是伟力的显现，是对于外来者的惩罚。

Maybe he had a point, I don't know. Maybe there's truly something greater out there, or maybe it's just what we mortals need to believe.
？？？：……好吧，见鬼。来吧，看看那帮以光荣与伟力自居的家伙都干了些什么好事儿。

These men from the Apostles Brotherhood used to be regular church-goers just like anybody else. But faced with an opportunity to rise above their own cruel realities, they all too readily turned away.
？？？：他们曾是具象的虔诚，天国的投影。漫无目的地行走耗尽了他们的心智，一旦新的神迹显现，他们便如释重负地低下头，将灵魂交由了新的主。

They left their old altars and took communion with a new god. Will this new path save them? Is it worth all their efforts? I can't say.
？？？：从雄壮的宗教支派沦为外来势力的附庸，这段短暂而可悲的历史为他们带来的唯一价值就是虚无的认同感，就像试图吞枪自尽的俘虏，发现敌人根本没给他留哪怕一小粒弹片。

But you wanted to know if this 'savior' can save its own. Well, I guess you've got your answer now.
？？？：冠以“天使”之名的并非真正的天使，他们无法向世人许诺任何东西，愚蠢至极。

选项 / Choices

1. Are we going to be alright?
1. 现在能救我了么？

No need to start panicking just yet. Things are still under control. Your lady friend has stepped up to solve this mess, and the veteran knows what he's doing.
？？？：不必担心，事态还在可控范围内，你并非无计可施。勇敢的绿裙小姑娘已经挺身而出，而半退休的老兵也有自己的谋划。

This city is unlike any you've seen before. You must learn its secrets to survive—remember which street kids are from which alley and what intel they can be relied on for, learn the dates when the dealers bring their "medicine" into port, and figure out which water tanks hide the key to the local gang's safehouse.
？？？：这地方和你待过的任何一座城市都不一样。你得看清并抓住能利用的一切——比如可作为情报员的孩童喜欢聚在哪儿踢球，比如交易药物的邮船常在几月份靠岸，比如哪只蓝色的蓄水缸下面藏着帮派安全屋的钥匙。

Don't look at me for answers. It's none of my business.
？？？：还有，提醒你一下，我不是你的助手，不会替你做这些事，你得自己去解决。

共同接续 / Shared continuation

Who is the Savior?
自救与拯救

14  河上的生与死 / On the Margins of Life and Death

On the Margins of Life and Death
河上的生与死

???: In the narrow streets, violence is so often more than just a means to ending conflicts. It is a tool, and one all too readily used.
？？？：暴力在这里并非解决纠纷的手段，而是一种习惯。

???: I must note, however, with some relief, that the segregation so commonly observed in other regions is absent here. The arcanists of São Paulo live alongside humans, with the latter often displaying traits more common to their arcanist neighbors—passionate, if a bit touchy.
？？？：神秘学家和人类混居在同一片区域，他们同样热情，也同样易于躁动。

???: Life here is incomparable to the comfort, security, and freedom of New England.
？？？：而遥远的北方是曾被称作新英格兰的繁华海岸。

???: Hardship, violence, and oppression are the fundamentals of life, as constant as the sun, and as heavy as the rain.
？？？：这里，则是一片暴力、苦难和流血纷争的土地。

???: This country is renowned for its coffee and sugar. It is a land of beauty and bounty, treasures too often extorted. Whether by the princes of Europe and their donatários, or now by the bankers and corporations.
？？？：这个国家因咖啡和蔗糖而闻名。过去的五个世纪里，从马德里的王公到热那亚的金融家，整个欧洲都曾享用其血肉。

???: Despite all the centuries of treasures extracted from the land, desperation remains everywhere you look. It is a place where insurmountable wealth and the deepest deprivations are neighbors—sometimes they lie only a street across from one another.
？？？：难以置信的巨富和同样令人不敢相信的绝对赤贫相依相伴，它们之间的距离有时仅仅只是一条街的宽度。

???: The multinationals have cut open the country's throat, and now they drink it dry—
？？？：跨国公司切开了拉丁美洲的血管，扎进针管，从中抽走它旺盛蓬勃的生命。

???: leaving the people struggling with poverty and chaos. It is a bleak existence.
？？？：留下混乱与血腥。

???: Dear readers, I share this with you, so the story of these people might be heard.
？？？：敬爱的读者们，这就是我正身处的土地。

Favela
河岸贫民区

The doctor sits by the riverside, a small group of children gathered around her. They watch in awe as her typewriter hammers the letters onto the page.
喧哗的河岸边，医生敲打着打字机。孩子们聚在她身旁，围观这个古怪的小机器。

Doctor Dores: Shippy, do you know, has life always been like this here?
德洛丝医生：小船，这儿的人一直都这样生活着吗？

White Rum: If you ask me, Doc, São Paulo in 1990 isn't much different from Nassau, 1681.
白朗姆：1681年的拿骚港和1990年的圣保罗，说真的，医生，在我看来就是一回事。

Doctor Dores: It's a shame. Between the sunshine and warm sands, there is a good life to be lived here, if only they were free enough to live it.
德洛丝医生：大自然对这儿很慷慨，充足的光照、热量和水，这本该是一片富饶的土地。

The summer is long, just like the suffering of these people.
漫长的夏日，如同这个国家漫长的苦难。

Doctor Dores: I used to dream of traveling to all sorts of fascinating places.
德洛丝医生：以前我总是会做一些模糊的梦……去到一些陌生的地方。

Doctor Dores: A foggy city by the Thames, and a little town blanketed in snow and sunflowers. I met so many different people, though I find I can no longer recall their voices, I have only their words left, only my records of them.
德洛丝医生：泰晤士河畔的雾都、一个很冷很冷的北国小镇……我还遇到了很多人，只是现在都记不起来了。

White Rum: Have you heard of a weed down at the bottom of Lake Ilopango? The people there turn it into a polenta with cornmeal.
白朗姆：噢，提到这个，圣萨尔瓦多岛的伊洛潘戈湖里有种水草，当地人把这东西采出来，伴着玉米做成糊糊，吃下去。

White Rum: Supposedly, if you have it, you'd recall every last thing that's ever happened to you. Every bit of it!
白朗姆：然后呢，然后……哈哈，然后你就会想起发生过的所有事！所有的事！

Kid in the Favela: Wow! What's the weed called?
贫民区小孩：哇啊……那是什么好宝贝！

The children turn to the ship in the bottle, their eyes filled with curiosity.
孩子们围坐在瓶中船“身”边，好奇地问着。

White Rum: They call it "La hierba del tonto," Spanish for "the fool's weed," ahahahaha!
白朗姆：喔，那东西叫白痴草，哈哈哈哈！

White Rum: You see, lad, only a fool would want to remember everything. Sometimes, a little forgetfulness is a blessing in disguise.
白朗姆：一个人要是什么都记得住，那这个倒霉的蠢蛋，离发疯就不远了。

Doctor Dores: I beg to differ, Shippy. To forget the past—its lessons, in pain and joy alike—that isn't a blessing. It's oblivion.
德洛丝医生：我倒是宁愿什么都能记得。遗忘很痛苦，小船。

Doctor Dores: But let's not argue over some mythical weed. For all the claims, no one has ever seen it, just another myth, like the succubus or the minotaur.
德洛丝医生：好了，羊癫草只是一个可爱的传说。小船，它像沙漠半岛里的魅魔传说一样，很多年都没人见过。

White Rum: Aye, aye, let's talk about succubi instead. That's sure to get the lads pricking up their ears!
白朗姆：噢，魅魔，哈哈，男人们听到这个，都得站起来！

At the mention of this, the little ship goes off again.
提到这个话题，瓶中船又开始胡说八道。

White Rum: Back in '88, I mean 1688, of course, our chief mate, Mr. Morgan, had an encounter with a succubus after he found her seal while carousing in Porto Belo.
白朗姆：88年——当然，我是说1688年，摩根大副在贝卢港捡到了某个魅魔的封印物，然后遇见了那该死的魅魔。

White Rum: If the rumors tell true, once he got a hold of it, she grabbed a hold on him too. I couldn't say what transpired, but we never saw hide nor hair of him again.
白朗姆：接下来，他失踪了一个月。好吧……我承认一个月有点夸大，但他确实死在贝卢港。

No one can debunk her stories, but no one really believes them either.
这儿没人能戳穿她的谎言，但也没人真的相信她的胡言乱语。

The children's curiosity fades, and their eyes start to wander. One of them spots two "big fish" floating in the water.
小孩子们漫不经心地听她讲故事，不时东张西望，意外瞧见水上漂来两条“大鱼”。

Kid in the Favela: Look over there! Something's in the water!
贫民区小孩：嘿，那边有人落水了！

Two figures float among the trash in the river, spluttering and choking.
两个浮在水上的人影。她们混在塑料瓶堆中间，呛着贫民区的脏水。

One is clinging onto the other as they struggle to swim to shore.
一个抱着另一个，正在奋力游向岸上。

White Rum: What the devil is all that commotion?
白朗姆：怎么回事……？

Vertin: ...
维尔汀：……

Ms. Kimberly: Hey. Wake up.
金伯利女郎：喂，快醒醒。

Ms. Kimberly: Hey, hey! Stay with me!
金伯利女郎：喂……喂！你可别淹死了！

Ms. Kimberly: Can you hear me? Hello?
金伯利女郎：喂……喂……

Kimberly pats her fallen companion's face.
金伯利拍拍落水伙伴的脸，试图叫醒她。

No response.
没有反应。

Ms. Kimberly: She's still breathing. Why won't she wake up?
金伯利女郎：明明还在呼吸啊……

White Rum: Oh. What happened to the lass?
白朗姆：呼……这是怎么一回事？

The little ship makes its way over, followed closely by the children and the doctor.
紧跟在小船身后赶来的，是盲眼医生和孩子们。

Seeing the group of strangers approaching, Kimberly tenses up.
看到一群陌生人赶来，金伯利略微警惕地耸起了肩膀。

Doctor Dores: Miss, are you alright? Is there anything we can do?
德洛丝医生：小姐，你还好吗？需要我的帮助吗？

Ms. Kimberly: Yes, I'm fine. Our ship went down.
金伯利女郎：没什么，只是船爆炸了。

White Rum: Blow me down! These waters are getting rougher by the day.
白朗姆：老天，我好好的，还没炸呢！

The little ship sways back and forth in its bottle, showing off its excellent condition.
瓶中船晃荡几下，示意自己的健康“无损”。

Ms. Kimberly: And we drifted down the river until we reached the shore here.
金伯利女郎：不，我说的可是一艘游轮！总之，我们都掉到了水里，一路漂向这儿。

A long way to go, only to end up in the favela.
从上游顺着水流漂下，来到这儿——一座贫民区。

Ms. Kimberly: I know she's traveling with her friends, but I don't know where they could be now. Everything happened so fast.
金伯利女郎：她应该还有个一起的朋友，但她不见了，我只能抱着她一起漂流。

Doctor Dores: Shippy, what's going on? Are there people that need care? What's their condition?
德洛丝医生：小船，落水的人情况怎么样？

White Rum: Just one. A lass. She's still breathing, but she looks like she's been keel-hauled for a bit too long.
白朗姆：还有呼吸，活着呢！不过……啧啧，恐怕也没那么乐观。

Doctor Dores: Miss, please lay her down on her side.
德洛丝医生：小姐，请把她放到地上，侧卧。

Kimberly does as she is told.
金伯利一一照做。

Ms. Kimberly: What are you going to do to her?
金伯利女郎：你要做什么……？

The doctor kneels down, skillfully clearing the girl's mouth of any residue before starting chest compressions.
医生熟练地跪下，把女孩口中的污秽掏出，确保呼吸通畅后，开始按压胸部。

Doctor Dores: Save her, if I can.
德洛丝医生：救她。

One push. Another.
一下，一下。

It's a professional instinct. The duty of a doctor.
这是职业本能，也是医者的神圣精神。

An unexplainable impulse drives her to revive the drowning girl.
一种不可思议的冲动催促着她对溺水的女孩施展救援。

Doctor Dores: Huh ... Huh ... Huh ... Huh ...
德洛丝医生：哈……呼……哈……呼……

The girl coughs up some water. The doctor continues.
溺水者开始呛出脏污的河水，医生继续帮她恢复呼吸。

Doctor Dores: Good, good. She'll make it. Please, make sure her airway's clear.
德洛丝医生：很快就会康复了，好孩子。没事的，保持呼吸。

She coughs out more water, but she still lies there, unable to move.
她慢慢咳出更多积水，但四肢乏力，躺在地上没法动弹。

White Rum: Errh, are you sure about that, Doc? The lass looks paler than a ghost. I think she's burning up.
白朗姆：我看事情没那么简单……她发烧了吗？

The doctor touches her forehead. It's burning hot.
医生伸手摸向额头——手背滚烫。

Doctor Dores: You're right. We'll need to take her back with us. I'll fix some medicine for her.
德洛丝医生：她发烧了……带她回去，得去弄点药。

Doctor Dores: Would you give me a hand, Miss? Help her up on my back.
德洛丝医生：帮帮忙，请把她抬到我背上。

Kimberly obediently helps the blind doctor lift the feverish girl onto her back.
金伯利没有多说什么，帮着盲眼医生背起了发烧的女孩。

Doctor Dores: Oh! She's almost as light as a child.
德洛丝医生：……她很轻。

The children run ahead, pulling aside the hanging clothes for them to pass.
孩子们跑在前边，为医生扯开拦路晾晒的衣服。

Doctor Dores: I need to run some tests and see if it's just an ordinary fever, or if she has contracted something from the water.
德洛丝医生：这不像是普通的发热，或许感染了河内的热毒菌。

Doctor Dores: Don't worry. You're safe with us.
德洛丝医生：别担心，我会帮助你的。

The doctor speaks to herself, or perhaps in reassurance to the unconscious girl.
医生自言自语，又或许是在安抚陷入昏迷的病人。

Ms. Kimberly: Where are we going? Wait up!
金伯利女郎：喂，等等我——

Doctor Dores: Home.
德洛丝医生：带她回家。

Kimberly catches up to the doctor, only now realizing how far they've walked.
瓶中船跟在身后，金伯利女郎追上医生——等回过神来，她发现，自己已经跟着走了很远。

She steps into the doctor's "home."
她走进了医生的“家”。

16  往日的分径-天使娜娜 / Paths of the Past - Anjo Nala

She used to comfort those childlike elders with a siren song that made them forget their pain and aged bodies.
她曾借此抚慰那些稚童般的老人，以那令人忘却止痛药与旧皮囊的塞壬之歌。

17  惊情三百年 / Aged Like Fine Wine

Aged Like Fine Wine
惊情三百年

A Shelter in the Favela
避难小屋

A fan whirs loudly, yet the room is just as hot as the outside.
电扇呼呼吹着，小屋里依然炎热，一似户外河岸。

The doctor gently lays the unconscious girl down, resting her head on her lap.
医生缓缓放下昏迷的病患，让那位小姐平稳躺上自己的大腿。

She lightly strokes her flushed face.
她轻抚着病患发烧滚烫的脸颊。

Doctor Dores: So you weren't even traveling together, and still you brought her all the way here?
德洛丝医生：所以……你们只是偶然相遇，然后你救了她。

Doctor Dores: You have a good heart, Ms. Kimberly.
德洛丝医生：谢谢你的善良，金伯利小姐。

Ms. Kimberly: You flatter me, Doctor. It was only that the current pushed her to my side when we all fell into the water.
金伯利女郎：不，别这么说我。河水正好把她冲到我身旁……

Ms. Kimberly: And I just couldn't forgive myself if I were to let her drown.
金伯利女郎：我不想看着她就这样沉下去。

An accident or, perhaps, fate.
一个意外——也可以叫它巧合。

Ms. Kimberly: In fact, she and I had met briefly before, in Texas. We didn't part on the best terms.
金伯利女郎：这是我们第二次见面，尽管上一回……上一回在德克萨斯，闹得不太愉快。

Doctor Dores: Sometimes, we find ourselves in quarrels, even among friends.
德洛丝医生：朋友之间难免会吵架。

Ms. Kimberly: No, we aren't ...
金伯利女郎：不，我们不是……

Vertin: ...
维尔汀：……

Doctor Dores: Shh. We should let her get some rest.
德洛丝医生：不好意思，我想我们的声音有些叨扰到她了。她需要休息。

Kimberly falls silent.
金伯利不再出声。

She squats down, watching her the way a cat peers at a sleeping baby.
她蹲了下来，就像摇篮边的猫，安静而好奇地望着熟睡的孩子。

Ms. Kimberly: May I touch her?
金伯利女郎：我可以摸一摸她吗？

Doctor Dores: Fever is one of the biggest dangers here, though it's still better than diarrhea and vomiting.
德洛丝医生：发烧在这儿很要命，但比腹泻和呕吐好。

Doctor Dores: I'll take her to the Veterans' Residence for further treatment once her condition stabilizes.
德洛丝医生：等情况好一点，我会带她去荣军院进一步治疗。

Ms. Kimberly: It almost looks like she's only sleeping.
金伯利女郎：她好像只是睡着了。

Doctor Dores: Are you two really not friends? Seems to me that you care for her very much.
德洛丝医生：你们真的不是朋友吗？

Ms. Kimberly: She helped me. She was kind to me on the ship. That bloody ship ... I don't know what happened.
金伯利女郎：她在船上帮了我。但那该死的船不知道为什么爆炸了！

Doctor Dores: Shh. Hush, please.
德洛丝医生：嘘……嘘……

The doctor presses a finger against her lips, a gentle smile on her face.
医生示意她安静，也温柔地对她展露笑容。

Ms. Kimberly: My apologies.
金伯利女郎：对不起，我忘了……

She has the face of a guilty child.
她像个做错事的孩子。

Ms. Kimberly: *sigh* As I said, it was only twice that we'd met, and somehow, both times turned out quite terrible for me.
金伯利女郎：我两次遇到维尔汀，两次都遇到了倒霉的事情。

The doctor strokes the feverish patient's hair. It's beautiful—almost the same color as hers.
医生抚摸着发烧病患的头发，柔顺、漂亮，跟她的发色几乎一样。

Doctor Dores: Her hair is so silky. It's smooth and soft, like fine rain in my hands.
德洛丝医生：她的头发像从天上流下的水。

The doctor gently combs the hair with her fingers, slowly untangling the matted strands.
医生细长的手指穿过发烧病患的发丝，一点点梳开被水黏住的头发。

Ms. Kimberly: First it was Tuesday's Motel. Things there turned into a right mess, and I didn't find what I'd lost.
金伯利女郎：第一次在蓝手帕旅馆，结果那儿的事情变得乱七八糟，我也没找到弄丢的东西。

Ms. Kimberly: Then, in that manor, just as I was about to enjoy a bit of dinner, that wild girl burst in. They caught me, threw me into a tower, and locked me up.
金伯利女郎：后来在庄园，我只是在那儿吃饭，天哪——冲进来一群人，把我绑走，扔到塔里关着。

Ms. Kimberly: I was stuck there, waiting like a lamb to be slaughtered.
金伯利女郎：我像只等着被杀的羊，不知道他们什么时候会把我送去屠宰场。

Doctor Dores: But you've made it through. You're safe with us.
德洛丝医生：但你都挺过来了。你很厉害，孩子。瞧，你现在在这间小屋里，这儿很安全。

Doctor Dores: No one is going to hurt you here.
德洛丝医生：没有人会伤害你。

Ms. Kimberly: Thank you, Doctor.
金伯利女郎：谢谢你，医生。

Kimberly gently takes the feverish girl's hand into her own. It's limp and slightly rough to the touch.
她握起病人垂下的手——一只没有气力的手、略有粗糙的手、微微发烫的手。

The blurred memory of her sister comes to mind, as well as the wrinkled, warm faces of her old friends cheering her on.
像是她模糊不定的姐姐，又像是那些欢呼雀跃的老朋友们皱巴巴而温暖的身影。

Ms. Kimberly: Goodbye.
金伯利女郎：……再见。

Satisfied that she has paid her back for her kindness on the ship, Kimberly leaves through the back door.
金伯利从后门退出房间，不再打扰医生。她已经“偿还”了船上得到的善意。她该离开了。

Ms. Kimberly: ?!
金伯利女郎：？！

The little ship in a bottle is waiting for her on the other side.
瓶中船等在门口。

White Rum: Leaving your friend so soon, lass?
白朗姆：我猜你是不是要走了？你说你不是她的朋友，那你们的这段旅途，我想该到此为止了。

White Rum: Well, come as you are, I'll give you a lift. Perhaps we might have a bit of an old jaw along the way.
白朗姆：来吧，亲爱的，我送你一程，路上也好聊聊。

White Rum: After all, it's rare indeed to see one of your kind out and about these days.
白朗姆：像你这样的……小姐，在这儿可不多见。

Ms. Kimberly: A-A lift? Uh, pardon me, madam, but, I'm not sure you could bring me anywhere.
金伯利女郎：我没想到……

Ms. Kimberly: You're a little too small, and a little too stuck in that bottle.
金伯利女郎：你……真的是艘船……

A galleon, born from the 16th century's dreams of exploration. She awakened and slumbered once again until now, a time in which there are no more uncharted lands to uncover.
一艘盖伦帆船，源于十六世纪的探索之梦。她因之苏醒，又因之长眠，长眠至世上再也没有处女地的千禧年前。

White Rum: Climb aboard, matey! So, where's our heading? Or shall we go for a ride on the river? Don't be shy. I aim to make a friend of you.
白朗姆：上来吧，如果你要走，我送你一程。如果你没打算离开，那就当是一个小小的邀请——交个朋友如何？

Kimberly looks hesitant.
金伯利犹豫着，迟迟没能下定决心踏上小小的甲板。

White Rum: Don't be shy. I let the little ones play on my deck all the time, climbing here and there, like a bunch of cheerful little monkeys.
白朗姆：孩子们都很喜欢在船上玩，像一群小猴子爬上桅杆，拉扯船帆。

White Rum: And not just them. I ferry the good doctor from here to the Veterans' Residence too.
白朗姆：医生往返荣军院和这边，都靠我接送。别担心。

White Rum: You'll have to wait till we reach the sea to see me at my full size.
白朗姆：如果在海上，你会见到一艘大得多的真家伙。

White Rum: Once the water opens up, that's when I'm really free.
白朗姆：没办法，这地方就这么窄。小瓶里装不了大船。水有多宽，船就能有多大。

The sailboat slowly drifts away from the doctor's hut.
帆船慢慢离开医生的小屋，顺流而去。

White Rum: Now, I ask again, where are we going, mate? Can't just let the wind decide our course.
白朗姆：你要去哪？总得有个方向，要是不知道自己去哪，吹什么风都没用。

Ms. Kimberly: Why not? I should like to go somewhere nobody will ever find me.
金伯利女郎：去哪儿都行，只要到一个没人找得到我的地方。

Confusion, chaos, escape—perhaps tomorrow will be just like all those yesterdays.
迷茫，混沌，逃亡，明天或许也像每一个远去的昨天一样。

White Rum: I'm afraid there's precious few places like that anymore, lass.
白朗姆：遗憾地说，我认为二十世纪九十年代的地球应该不存在这样的地方了。

White Rum: I've spied the globes they've got nowadays; seems like every last step is covered to the four corners and back.
白朗姆：我看过最新的地球仪，天哪，他们连亚马孙雨林里哪儿有个水坑都知道。

White Rum: But aren't you a strange sight, lass? You know I've seen your kind before. Beautiful to look at, aye, but there's a darkness in you that lies beneath the surface, isn't there?
白朗姆：宝贝儿，哪里能找到一只愿意好心救助陌生人的小魅魔呢？我见过你的同胞，她们恶劣、贪婪又下流，但个个都是十足的尤物。

White Rum: But you're not like the rest of them, you'd say. Well, I would sooner believe in a Map to El Dorado before I'd swallow the idea of a succubus with a heart of gold.
白朗姆：而一个会冒险救下陌生人的魅魔……那我宁愿相信埃尔多拉多不是一个传说。

White Rum: So, was that girl your master? Did she have your seal?
白朗姆：我猜，她是你的主人，对不对？或者……她拿着你的封印物？你不得不听命于她？

White Rum: If that were true, then heart of gold or coal, wouldn't matter for none. You'd be as thick as old tar.
白朗姆：如果她真是你的主人，那你恐怕是我见过的最蠢的小魅魔。

She's been found out. She's a member of an ancient race once wanted—even hunted—and now forgotten by the world.
她被认出来了——一个被通缉、被追捕、被遗忘于世人记忆中的古老种族。

A few seagulls land on the boat's edge, joining them on their journey.
水鸟停靠在船沿，加入旅途。

Ms. Kimberly: ...?!
金伯利女郎：……？！

White Rum: What, are you wondering what gave you away? Your gloves don't do much to hide those claws. I could feel them pressing on my railings as soon as you hopped on.
白朗姆：魅魔小姐，你应该给自己弄一只更好的手套，这样才能遮住爪子。

Kimberly immediately hides her hands behind her back.
金伯利立刻将手藏到身后。

Her reaction betrays her.
她的反应出卖了自己。

White Rum: Ahahahaha! No use hiding them now, matey.
白朗姆：哈哈，看来是真的！

Her hearty laugh sends ripples through the water.
帆船大笑着荡起河浪。

Ms. Kimberly: You!
金伯利女郎：喔呜——！

Kimberly scratches the mast with her claws, but this small protest does nothing to dissuade the ship.
金伯利对着桅杆挠下一道抓痕，但她小小的抗议在帆船看来却无足轻重。

Ms. Kimberly: Are you going to turn me in?
金伯利女郎：你也是来抓我的吗？

She looks around. This place looks safe enough. Just one small leap, and she'd be back on solid ground.
她环顾四周。这儿很安全，只要轻轻一跳，她就能回到最近的地面。

White Rum: Turn you in? And how do you suppose I do that! I'm only a ruddy ship! Bring me back up on land, and I'll be stuck right back in the bottle.
白朗姆：抓你？天哪，小甜心！我只是一艘船呀，被我那亲爱的船长放进瓶子里，一睡睡了将近三百年的小船。

White Rum: No, lass. I've sailed with rougher folks than you. My old captain had a bounty on her head, too.
白朗姆：和你一样，她也是位逃犯。

She's a fugitive, hiding like a frightened bird.
如同惊弓之鸟般四处躲藏的逃犯。

Thunder rumbles in the distance, scattering the birds perched on the roofs on either side of the river.
远方传来雷声，停驻在屋檐上的雀崽儿四散奔逃。

Ms. Kimberly: ...?!
金伯利女郎：……！

It makes her quiver, but for the boat, it brings back fond memories of sailing the roaring westerlies with her captain.
雷声令她紧张，却让身下帆船回想起曾随船长穿行咆哮西风带的旧日过往。

Rain starts to pour. The wind picks up, filling the ship's sails and racing her down the river.
响雷之后，是夏日的骤雨。河风吹鼓了船帆，小船兴奋地提高了速度。

Ms. Kimberly: Well, just so you know, I very much doubt they'll pay you anything for handing me over!
金伯利女郎：不会有人付你赏金的！

White Rum: Maybe so, maybe so. Now, you've got the law in your tailwind? What happened? Did you spring loose from the old gibbet?
白朗姆：喔，原来你真的是逃犯。

Ms. Kimberly: I ...
金伯利女郎：你……！

White Rum: Mark me now, young lass. If you're going to try to pull one over on me, you'll have to do a lot better job than that.
白朗姆：小姐，想瞒过别人，你应该先学会欺骗自己。

That same old trick.
故技重施。

Ms. Kimberly: Are you mocking me? This is not how one ought to treat a new friend.
金伯利女郎：我不喜欢被人捉弄的感觉。

White Rum: Quite right, lass. So then, who are your pursuers? And your master? Shouldn't you be under his protection? My old crew would say any captain that can't keep his ship safe is only good for bait.
白朗姆：好吧，我为这个恶作剧道歉。你在逃避谁？你的主人呢？他不会为你摆平麻烦吗？船长如果不能照顾他的船，他就该被丢海里去喂鱼。

Ms. Kimberly: I have no idea where he is, or where my seal is for that matter—he was a no-good fool, though. Or only good for bait, perhaps.
金伯利女郎：我不知道。那家伙是个傻子，还弄丢了我的封印物。该死。

Ms. Kimberly: I hope he's dead, rotting in some sewer back in San Francisco.
金伯利女郎：但愿他早点完蛋，死在旧金山哪个没人知道的下水道里最好。

The rain stops. Brief and intense, just like her escape.
一场短促的雨，正如她短促而匆忙的逃亡——雨势渐大，来得快，去得也一样快。

White Rum: Aye, well, mortal flesh doesn't last long. I've ferried many a passenger in my days, and none yet have escaped when Davy Jones came calling their name.
白朗姆：别担心，这艘船载过很多人，虽然他们无一例外，最后全都死了——但谁又能一直活着呢？

White Rum: As I recall, the first that went was William Half-Leg. He lost the other half in a fight at the port of Southampton. Hanged on the governor's orders.
白朗姆：“断腿”威廉被吊死前是个纵火犯，一嘴烂牙，在南安普敦上船前被仇家锯断了右腿。

White Rum: And Eduard the Red-Hand, a nasty piece of work from the low countries, loved a bit of throat-cutting. Until he got his own throat slit by some fool in a ramshackle pub on Tortuga. Hahahahaha!
白朗姆：“红手”爱德华是低地佬，喜欢用钝刀子割人质喉咙，但他却在托尔图加港被一个醉鬼抹了脖子。

She fondly remembers every one of her crew. Though they all died hundreds of years ago, it still feels like yesterday to her.
她一一回忆自己的伙伴。事情久远，但都像发生在昨天。见多识广的小船记得她的每一位乘客。

Ms. Kimberly: Sounds as though they got what they deserved.
金伯利女郎：这些人肯定都没有好下场。

White Rum: That they did, lass. They all got what they deserved. Some hanged, some drowned, some we marooned on a desert isle with naught but a pistol and a flagon of rum.
白朗姆：当然，每个人都死了。吊死的，淹死的，被船上的伙计们丢去荒岛上等死的……

White Rum: I've been to Plymouth, Nassau, Port Royal. I once even slipped into Seville disguised as a merchantman to smuggle pepper and cinnamon. Wherever the captain wanted to go, we sailed.
白朗姆：有船长的时候，我跟着船长的号令走，去普利茅斯，去拿骚，去皇家港……甚至装成商船在塞维利亚偷运胡椒和肉桂。

An exciting, dangerous life, yet ordinary in its own way.
一段惊险的岁月，惊险而又平平无奇。

The countless cannonball scars in her hull alone tell the story.
像十八磅加农炮留在她身上的无数个弹孔一样危险。

White Rum: But one by one, Davy took them all. Now here I am, a ship without a captain. My own master.
白朗姆：但现在，他们全都没了。可船永远是这艘船，没有船长，我也照样出航，想去哪儿就去哪儿。

White Rum: I suppose that's a lot to take in. You ever thought about living a different life?
白朗姆：我知道这对一个生来要服从契约的魅魔来说很难理解，但你有没有想过……过上另一种生活呢？

White Rum: To set sail for some far corner of the sea, living on spiced rum and jerky thick as a buccaneer's hide?
白朗姆：喝着朗姆酒，嚼着比国王脸皮还厚的硬肉干，出海寻找那些没人见过的小岛。

White Rum: How's that sound, lass? Not that I'm saying we set sail today. It's only a bit of old fancy.
白朗姆：我的意思是……好奇，就是好奇，哈哈，我也很好奇你，所以才在这儿打听。

Ms. Kimberly: I really can't say, uhm, Shippy. I, I suppose I can't, not yet at least. There is something important I need to retrieve.
金伯利女郎：我不知道，小船。那可能不重要，我得先……得先找到我弄丢的重要的东西。

White Rum: Ah-ah, that's "Captain White Rum" to you—the ship of a captain, and captain of the ship!
白朗姆：请叫我“船长”——伟大的白朗姆号！既是船长的船，也是船的船长！

Ms. Kimberly: I prefer ... Wait ... Sorry ... I need to ...
金伯利女郎：我可能得……等等……我有点儿……想吐……

Favela
河岸贫民区

As the ship docks, she leaps back ashore, doubles over in an alleyway, and retches.
小船靠岸，她跳回岸上，撑着棚屋干呕。

White Rum: Seasick? I hadn't thought your kind capable of it.
白朗姆：……你没说过你会晕船。

Ms. Kimberly: I didn't expect this ... either ...
金伯利女郎：你也没跟我说……河上会……这么晃……啊……

A can rolls over, gently hitting her foot. Annoyed, she kicks it away.
她脚边滚来一个铝罐，打断了和小船的闲聊。金伯利不耐烦地把这小玩意儿踢走。

Not far away, a few boys have been practicing their soccer skills with it. One mischievous boy kicks the can back toward Kimberly.
不远处，几个少年用铝罐代替足球，练习着颠球。某个好事的小伙子又一脚将“球”踢向金伯利。

Ms. Kimberly: Urgh! What now?
金伯利女郎：该死的，什么东西？

This time, she picks up the can and angrily throws it at the boy's head.
这一次，金伯利捡起铝罐，气冲冲地扔向那小伙子的脑袋。

Teenager in the Favela: Jeez! I like this girl. She's feisty!
贫民区少年：你敢朝我扔东西？

He straightens himself up as he slowly makes his way toward her, a cocky grin on his face.
面对“还击”，好勇斗狠的少年挨了痛，却首先环顾自己周围的小伙计——和他一样茫然，一样不知所措。

Teenager in the Favela: We're with the Brotherhood, Miss. You apologize now, and we might just let you go.
贫民区少年：你敢对我们天使兄弟会的人动手！

Teenager in the Favela: Or don't you know that Lord Santos is back? We can make this rougher for you, if you like.
贫民区少年：桑托斯大人回来了！你完了！

White Rum: Careful me lads. I wouldn't pull those knives. Things may go rougher than you'd bargained for.
白朗姆：刀？小家伙们，这可不明智呀，嘿——

The boys are no match for her.
她轻松摆平了这些不自量力的小伙子。

Ms. Kimberly: Not so tough now, are you boys? Try this again, and I'll make a mince out of what's left of you!
金伯利女郎：哼，龇牙咧嘴的小鬼，再让我看到你们，我一定揪出你们脑子里的毛病来！

She angrily kicks the can into the river.
赶走上来挑事的孩子后，她撒气般将脚边的瓶罐一一踹入河中。

White Rum: I know your fathers, me boys, and I think they wouldn't be none too happy to hear you claiming to be with the Brotherhood.
白朗姆：我想这是个误会……我认识你们的老爹，要是让他知道你们说自己是兄弟会的人，恐怕你们都得挨顿打。

Teenager in the Favela: Let's go. We're wasting our time here.
贫民区少年：……切，走了走了，没意思。她不会踢球，我们不跟她玩。

The boys scurry off, muttering under their breaths.
自称兄弟会信众的少年被吓得尿了裤子，放下狠话，拉着伙伴匆忙逃离。

White Rum: Bilge rats, the lot of them. Not even fit for the Apostles.
白朗姆：我敢保证兄弟会的人根本不认识他们。

White Rum: The Brotherhood always let their knives do the talking.
白朗姆：那伙人能动刀子解决问题，就绝不会动嘴皮子。

White Rum: If there'd been any doubt of what you are, lass, that fight sure cleared it up.
白朗姆：另外……我现在确信你真是个魅魔了，小姐。你下手真的没有轻重。
